- **🛠️ PROGRESSION IMPLEMENTED — 4 server commits + 1 game commit (agent `council-v2`, 01:37→02:00)** — User: *"ok now lets implement everything, front end back end etc."* All TDD (test written first, watched fail, then implemented). **Nothing pushed.**
  - **`pikme-server` `85619ee` — record-match was UNAUTHENTICATED.** It ran `authNonBlock`, which sets `req.userId = null` on a bad/absent token and calls `next()` anyway, then took identity from `req.body.phone`. So every economy control was a knob the caller types: **119 forged `vsHuman:true` wins reached LEGEND with a sticky floor in ~18s.** Now `authFootballStrict` + `phoneMatchesToken` (you can't post wins onto a phone your token doesn't own — needed because footballstats is phone-keyed while tokens carry a userId). Also bounded the `recordedMatchIds` `$push`, which could grow a doc to Mongo's 16MB cap and permanently brick that account's writes. **No iOS change needed** — the app already sends the login token via `headers.auth` (`services/http.js`). Tests: `test-football-record-auth.mjs` (17).
  - **`pikme-server` `3bf4290` — trophy math (asks #1–#4).** `PLAYED 20 + WIN 80 / TIE 30 / LOSS 10 + 10×min(goals,5)`, × stepped roster grade, × `meetTaper`, × `botTaper`, `+200` first win/day. Pre-roster totals are **byte-identical to shipped 100/50/30**, so ask #2 is four visible line items at zero curve cost. **Ask #3 was genuinely unimplemented**: `xpFactor` graded 0.20/0.47/0.73/1.00 but `computeMatchXp` clamped to [0.5,1.0], so 0.47 clamped UP and a 2v2 with one human paid exactly what 3 bots paid. `rosterRate` now inverts the shipped encoding → 0.50/0.65/0.80/1.00, a formula not a table, so 2v2 + 3v3 + 1v1 are all covered with **no new wire field**. New schema fields `botXpDate`/`botXpToday`. Tests: `test-football-trophies.mjs` (75) — they caught a real IEEE754 bug (`0.60−0.20 = 0.39999999999999997` dropped an exact-half roster a whole grade).
  - **`pikme-server` `d12ac9d` — BOTH badges violated rule #1.** Executed: `tierFromStats({xp:40000,wh:100,wb:99})` → champion, `{wb:101}` → **diamond** — two bot wins demoted a champion. `tierFromRank` had it too, and the sticky floor could NOT protect it (`applyRankDelta` floors the POINTS, `tierFromRank` gated the INDEX afterwards). And `wh<25` froze a bots-only account at **gold at any total** (`{xp:1000000}` → gold), which became fatal once trophies were the solo player's whole ladder. Both gate sets deleted. Tests: `test-football-badges.mjs` (15 failures confirmed first).
  - **`pikme-server` `566996b` — rank is HUMANS-ONLY (the 01:19 ruling).** Bot matches pay **0**, no gain and no loss, at every difficulty and standing. `BOT_RATE`/`BOT_DAILY_CAP`/`botCeiling` zeroed (not deleted — `BOT_RATE` was also the mixed-roster floor, and the game mirrors them under the parity test). Rank now shares the **same** `rosterRate` as trophies. Tests: `test-football-rank-humans.mjs` (20 failures first); **24 assertions in `test-football-rank.mjs` rewritten in place**, including the end-to-end story that used to ask "how far can bots carry a player".

- **⚖️ RANK IS NOW EVENT-ONLY + UPSETS PAY MUCH MORE (user rulings to agent `council-v2`, 01:19 + 01:22)** — ⚠️ **These supersede `summery/research-trophies/00-DECISION.md` §7.1. Read this before touching any rank code.**
  - **Rank is earned ONLY in ranked games.** Ranked is a **special EVENT mode, humans only**. Quick match, play-with-friends and bot matches pay **trophies only** and never touch rank. The user reaffirmed it after being shown the solo-player cost — it is the industry norm (Fortnite, Rocket League and Brawl Stars all exclude bots from ranked) and it is his call.
  - **Therefore DEAD on the rank track:** `BOT_RATE` (0.4), `BOT_DAILY_CAP` (150), `botCeiling`/`atBotCeiling` (60+80·L), `applyFarmGates`. Also remove the trophy-side gates in `football-xp.js tierFromStats` (`wh < 25 → cap gold`) — they only existed because rank was bot-earnable, and they are what freezes a solo player at gold at *any* trophy total (verified by execution: `{xp:1000000, wh:0}` → gold).
  - **Upsets must pay MUCH more** — verbatim: *"if a lower rank say level 1 wins two level 10 then he should progress much more then beating two level 1."* Target ~4× for a big upset, inverse on the downside. Note for whoever implements: **pure Elo cannot do this** — its multiplier is bounded at 2× by construction — so it must be a gap-keyed table, not an Elo term.
  - **Trophies are now the solo player's entire ladder**, so rank needs a new **`לא מדורג` / unranked** state (today everyone starts bronze, which would now be a lie). Existing seeded rank (up to 900 via `seedRankFromXp`) is **grandfathered — nobody is reduced** — it just stops moving until they play ranked.
  - **Why this unblocked #5/#7:** opponent-strength scaling was unshippable because nothing in today's pipeline knows anyone's rank (`opponentKey` is an irreversible sha256, `matchStart` carries no ranks, football-mock makes zero outbound calls to pikme-server). A ranked queue is **new plumbing**, so rank claims ride the wire from day one and quick-match is never touched.
  - **Tension flagged to the user:** tight ranked matchmaking would *prevent* upsets from ever happening, so the ranked matchmaker must be deliberately LOOSE. At a few-hundred-player base the queue is thin anyway, so wide gaps will be normal — meaning the upset bonus fires often, not rarely, and may need a per-event cap.

- **🔧 BOT OVERHAUL — SHIPPED locally (agent `bot-fix`, 01:09-03:0x)** — User: "fix everything... 1) a range of bot skills from very dumb to very very smart. 2) make sure the bot knows how to use the tricks: bomb correctly, hiding in bushes, passing to each other, building walls in strategic places, putting a bomb near a wall to fly further. Use the council of 5 with one challenger."
  - **Method:** council of 5 seats + 1 adversarial challenger each + an integrator (11 agents). **18 of 56 seat decisions were REFUTED, 33 amended** — two of the refutations hit code I had already written (see "corrected" below). I was the single writer; seats were read-only. Full council output: `/private/tmp/claude-501/.../tasks/wque7gkeb.output` (may be GC'd — the durable summary is here).
  - **5 commits:** `00794e9` lane/wind-up/cannon-spot · `228935f` seeded RNG · `3d08e27` goal-mouth + fixtures · `2103ae9` routing + release ladder · `64e0218` de-cheat + ladder + tricks. **Suite 57/57 green. NOT pushed, NOT deployed.**
  - **NEW TESTS (run these first if you touch bots):** `test-bot-stall.mjs` (8 capability fixtures for the two reported symptoms), `test-bot-ladder.mjs` (does the ladder RANK — Spearman rho, 30 matches/anchor, ~25s), `test-bot-tricks-fire.mjs` (per-tier trick histogram; FAILS if a user-named trick is silently dead).
  - **THE HEADLINE FINDING — the ladder was INVERTED.** Measured at 30 matches/anchor: the DUMBEST tier had the best goal differential, the top the worst (Spearman **rho -0.90**). Cause: `fireAt` clamped every ball release to FULL_CHARGE 0.71, which rolls **499px** (roll is linear in release speed, `0.468*(v0-18)`), while `FINISH_RANGE` was `560+220*AGG` = up to **835px**. Bots shot from ~300px beyond the ball's reach; the shot died short and gifted possession — and since the range scales with **aggro**, the smarter tiers wasted MORE. Now: a ball release fires at the charge it asked for, `FINISH_RANGE` derives from `ballRollPx()`, and each shot's charge is sized to the distance. **rho is now +0.90**, bottom tier went from strongest to weakest, spread 0.83 goals/match.
  - **"Stuck at a steel wall" — three compounding defects.** (a) `steer()` clamped to each wall's **AABB**; `capsuleAABB` of a 600x120 wall at 45° is 509x509, so 8px inside that corner a bot read 0px clearance where the truth is ~289px — a phantom obstacle on every angled/field-builder wall. Now exact `nearestOnWall`. (b) danger was proximity-based so tangential dirs scored WORSE than head-on (toward -1.2 / along -1.36 / retreat -1.0 → retreat wins → oscillation). Now a 3-sample RAY-CAST judged **relative to `c0`**, the clearance where the bot already stands — when wedged c0≈0 makes tangents look blocked too, which is what inverted the ranking. (c) exact clearance then made the two tangents perfectly symmetric → the 0.55 low-pass averaged them to zero → deadlock; added a **committed detour side** (`wallCommit`). **Local steering still could not do it** (it rounded a 600px wall then orbited the corner forever), so there is now a **32px occupancy grid + 8-connected BFS flow field**, cached per arena, LRU 16, engaged only when the field says the direct line is a detour. Cost **0.49ms/tick at 10 bots + 24 walls** (2.9% of a 60Hz frame); grid rebuilt once per arena (3.6ms only on the tick a wall is built — TODO: incremental re-stamp ≈20µs).
  - **"Stands with the ball in front of the goal" — the anti-idle release was gated on `laneWalls`,** so a wall across the lane left NO release path: the carrier drove into it and held the ball for the rest of the match. Now an ordered ladder that always terminates: 3 aim points (centre + both posts — only the CENTRE was ever tested), else smash a FRAGILE wall, else outlet-pass, else work the angle; plus a watchdog. The watchdog widens the **aim TOLERANCE**, because `fire` needs charge >= fireAt AND aim within tol, and it was tol that stalled — `shoot=true` alone would not release the ball.
  - **`laneClear` was lying in both directions:** fixed 10-step sampling meant a 100px stride on a 1000px lane, so a 32px built wall fell between samples ~2/3 of the time. Now exact segment-vs-AABB (slab), which is also CHEAPER, and it reports `out.wall` so callers can tell destructible from stone.
  - **Goal mouth:** `sim.js:842` implements a **DRIBBLE-IN GOAL** (#9) — walking the ball over the line scores, bypassing the kick/save path entirely, i.e. unsaveable and the best finish in the game. bot-ai.js's header claim "you cannot dribble a goal" is **STALE**, and the boundary danger repelled carriers at x>1945 while the walk-in needs x>1971. Boundary is now measured against a mirror of the sim's own clamp — deliberately `clampBallCarryXY` (carrier's attacking pocket only), NOT `clampXYToArea`: opening the pocket to every bot measurably raised pinning (0.29%→0.40%) because a pocket is a 300x70 dead end.
  - **TOO STRONG → strong by DECISIONS, not cheating** (Brawl Stars' model): **x-ray DELETED**; **permanent super DELETED** (it re-granted `p.power`+`powerUses` every tick they were false); **card/skill double-dip broken** (`RARITY_BY_LEVEL` hands the best cards exactly where the skill vector spikes and the sim multiplied both → difficulty grew ~quadratically; cards are now divided out at the write site); **fairness ceiling** capping effective charge rate at 2.50 → fastest full charge **0.57s (0.28s worst case) vs 0.15s before**; the t>=0.95 **cliff is now a ramp from 0.92** so L9 keeps zero of it; preCharge probabilistic + capped at `_charge<0.35` so a telegraph survives; `visionMul` made MONOTONE (hard was 1.90 > the new top).
  - **TRICKS THAT NEVER FIRED.** `carryJump` **DELETED — physically impossible**: `sim.js:840` gates the bomb on `!carrying`, so a carrier can never plant; it only froze the carrier on a fuse for a bomb that never spawned, **~50s/match** at the top tiers. `doubleBomb` was **geometrically impossible** (join gate 273px vs a >=320px separation floor) → widened to 462px. Plays gated on `toolSkill >= 0.9` were unreachable for the human's **PARTNER** (partner side is skill 0.05-0.82 → toolSkill 0.39-0.97) — that is the "my team-mate does nothing" half; lowered to 0.70-0.72. **RESTORED "bomb near a wall to fly further"** as a MOBILITY play only (never the tackle-steal — that is why the old nudge was deleted, 0% steal), matching the sim's real `wallCannonMul`: wall within 150px **BEHIND** the launch, ±35° cone, nothing indestructible ahead or the sim cancels the jump; steel peaks 1.55x. Measured spread now **5 distinct tricks at t=0.05 → 20 at t=0.82**, with `wallCannonJump`/`cannonSetup` appearing.
  - **⚠️ CORRECTED MID-FLIGHT (the challengers caught me):** (1) I wrote a `breakWall` branch on the belief that a carrier's full kick destroys a built wall. **It does not** — `damageWall` is called ONLY from the bullet path (`sim.js:1170`) and the bomb blast; a kicked ball just ricochets (`sim.js:895`). Only a **FRAGILE** (hp1) wall smashes, above `FRAGILE_PASS_SPEED`. Clearing a solid wall is the SUPPORT bot's job with a bullet. (2) My first watchdog could not actually release the ball (see the tolerance note above). (3) A TDZ crash (`ballR` used before its `const`) — same class as the TestFlight crash in this repo's history.
  - **⚠️ STILL OPEN / DO NOT ASSUME DONE:**
    - **`decisionHz` is STILL DEAD, deliberately.** It is the biggest remaining "feel dumb" lever, but I tried three cheap wirings and **each made the ladder measurably WORSE** (rho +0.70 → +0.20 → -0.50; shots/match -56%). The in-code comment records why: `carryT`/`blindT`/the progress ratchet are INTEGRATORS that must advance every tick, and 8 branches early-return into `finalize()` with hand-built aims. A correct revival = hoist the integrators + forceNow on role/possession change + live aim overrides. Council spec step 16B. **Don't repeat the wrapper attempts.**
    - **`goalScreen` and `doubleBomb` still measure 0 commits.** Wall budget/priority (council step 13) not done.
    - **Real PASSING (council step 10)** not done — passes work but are not the 4-type system with an honest reach cap. `outletPass` dominates the histogram.
    - **Bots never send `buildDist`,** so `p.aimMag` is always 0 (`sim.js:719`) → every bot wall places at the minimum 60px offset, never the 180px reach a human gets.
    - **Nothing verified on the phone or in the browser** — headless only. The ladder numbers are 2v2 bot-vs-bot; a human plays differently.
    - Levels may want re-cutting now that the inversion is fixed (council step 17); `xpDiffLevel` browser-testability (step 18) not done, so **the browser at 10.100.102.36 is still always difficulty level 0**.

Locks held on bot-ai/difficulty/sim throughout. `public/client.js` + `public/net-hud.js` had another agent's edits — left untouched.
  - **Measurement note:** `bot-eval.mjs` had counted `inputs[id].shoot`, a key `computeBotInputs` has never emitted, so "shots" read **0 in every run ever done**. And the harnesses ran unseeded, so identical code reported wall-pinning from 0.27% to 0.51% — I added `state.rng`/`SEED=` first, because without it none of the A/B numbers above would mean anything.

- **🐛 FIXED — the game said «חיבור לא יציב» while his internet was fine (agent `session-open-2`)** — User: "i see on the host connection unstable of the internet but my internet is fine, what is this connection?"
  - **Answer to the question:** it never measured his ISP. It measures the **WebSocket to the game server** — RTT from our own `ping`/`pong`, jitter over the last 8 round trips, snapshot arrival rate, the gap since the last snapshot, and the unacked-input backlog. In the browser that server is **his laptop on the LAN** (`10.100.102.36:3012`); on TestFlight it is **Render**. A perfect home internet connection says nothing about either.
  - **Both causes were OUR bugs, not the network. Reproduced on a synthetic 3ms zero-jitter link.**
    1. **Stale snapshot rate at kickoff.** `snapRate` came from `setInterval(..., 1000)` started at **page load** — a fixed-phase sampler, so its published value was up to a second old, and at kickoff that stale value was the lobby's **0**. `0 < NET_T.poor.snapRate (30)` → after the 600ms escalate dwell the toast fired. Measured: **false 'poor' at 601ms on a flawless link.** It hit whenever a match began >600ms before the next tick — roughly 40% of kickoffs.
       - Fix: a **rolling 1s window over real arrival times**, reporting **`null` (= unknown, ignored) until it holds a full second**, and **re-warming after any gap longer than the window**. That last part matters: the socket outlives a match, so without it the *second* match's first frames read 1/s, 2/s, 3/s and cried wolf again.
    2. **The warning outlived the pitch.** `renderFrame()` early-returns when the game screen is hidden, so whatever the last in-game frame painted — lit bars, the toast, the full-screen «מתחבר מחדש…» spinner — **froze on top of the hub with no frame coming to clear it.** New `hideNetHud()`, called from `showScreen()` on every change away from `'game'`.
  - **A genuine problem is still caught** — a sustained 10/s still reads 10/s and still classifies poor. The fix suppresses "I don't know yet", not "it's bad".
  - **Files I took:** `public/client.js` (rate window + the `showScreen` hook + import), `public/net-hud.js` (`hideNetHud`), NEW `test-net-warmup.mjs`. Commit **`61b6fb7`**, local only, **not pushed**.
  - **Verified:** 28 new checks — and they run the **real** rolling-window functions sliced out of `client.js` rather than a replica (a replica would only test itself). Full suite **48 PASS / 1 FAIL** (`test-mode-format.mjs`, needs `:3013`, squatted by a live dev server — pre-existing). `:3012` restarted; the fix is in the `client.js`/`net-hud.js` the browser actually downloads.
  - ⚠️ **Still worth watching on the phone.** If TestFlight shows «בינוני» against **Render**, that may be honest: `NET_T.fair.rtt` is **110ms**, and mobile→cloud RTT of 110-150ms is normal for a hosted game. The thresholds were tuned for a 60Hz LAN sim. **Don't retune them blind** — read the actual ping in ⚙ → 📶 חיבור first, which now prints the number and which threshold is failing.
  - ℹ️ **There is deliberately no packet-loss %** — the transport is WebSocket, i.e. TCP, which retransmits. Loss never appears as a missing packet at our layer; it appears as jitter and burst gaps. A loss counter would read 0 forever. (Fortnite can show one because it runs on UDP.)

  - **"The field" = an arena layout, not pitch dimensions** (that's what `field` means in this repo — the field-builder save shape). New **`shared/field-3v3.js`** (`FIELD_3V3`, "מגרש שלושות"): open central channel, cover pushed out to the three spawn lanes, midfield lane dividers, breakable crate screens flanking the centre. Reason: MAIN_FIELD's centre is dense (two bush blocks + a 12-crate column across x=1000) — fine for 4 bodies, a scrum with 6. Also registered in `FIELD_PRESETS` as **שלושות**, so it opens in the builder.
  - **Three things were silently broken and are now fixed** (all were in `summery/TEAM_FORMATS_PLAN.md` as blockers):
    1. **`MAX_PLAYERS = 4` was a module constant** → a 3v3 room capped at 4. Now per-room: `room.teamSize` + `roomMax(room)`/`roomTeamSize(room)`, set from the FORMATS row. All 14 call sites converted; private/training rooms keep the old default.
    2. **`spawnPos` was `slot === 0 ? .36 : .64`** → every slot past the first spawned on ONE spot, so 3v3 put two players inside each other. Now a formation: 2v2 keeps the **exact** original 0.36/0.64 pair (asserted byte-for-byte), 3+ spreads across a 0.18–0.82 band with the centre lane a touch further upfield. Reads `state.teamSize`.
    3. **`assignRoles` only had jobs for two bots** (`bots[0]`/`bots[1]`) → the 3rd bot per team got no role and ran the same support tactics as the 2nd, stacking on it. Added a **COVER lane** role: off-ball bots past the support hold distinct vertical lanes goal-side of the ball, still contesting anything inside 340px. `support` is now the off-ball bot nearest the ball (same pick as before at 2v2, so **2v2 is unchanged** — asserted: 0 cover lanes at 2v2).

Now every card resolves through `formatForCard()` → `applyFormat()`, one place that owns what a format means (win rule + team size + arena).

- **🧪 Bot difficulty REVIEW (no code changed) (agent `bot-review`, 00:56)** — User: bots feel too strong sometimes, too dumb sometimes; they get stuck in front of an obstacle (steel wall); sometimes they stand with the ball in front of the goal. Asked for a review of bot logic / tricks / difficulty levels only, no edits.
  - **BIGGEST FINDING: the browser and the phone run DIFFERENT difficulty.** `xpDiffLevel()` (client.js:4226) derives the level from `window.SALTIZ_XP`; `DEV_LOCAL` only covers localhost/127.0.0.1 (client.js:804), so at **10.100.102.36 there is no XP → level 0** (weakest tier, both sides 0.05). TestFlight DOES inject real XP (pikmeTV-saltiz football.jsx:174) → possibly the cheat tier (starts at 5,500 XP). Browser tuning tests a tier no player sees.
  - **"Too dumb" #1 — the wind-up can barely finish at low tiers.** time-to-fire = 1.42/chargeRate; level 0/1 chargeRate ~0.67 → **2.12s** vs the hard 2.2s abort (bot-ai.js:1155). 80ms margin; on cancel `p._charge` resets to 0 (sim.js:737) and it re-arms → holds the ball and never shoots. Shot tempo across the ladder: 2.1s → 0.15s (14×).
  - **"Too dumb" #2 — `decisionHz` is a DEAD knob** (in all tiers + SKILL_KEYS, never read). All bots decide at 60Hz; the only handicaps are aim slew + reaction, applied to AIM only — never to movement or the shoot decision.
  - **"Stuck at a wall" — steering weights, no pathfinding.** `steer()` (bot-ai.js:319) is local context steering; there is NO pathfinding in the repo. `score = interest − 2.2·danger` with one probe at look=110 and a 67px danger radius: pinned mid-wall, along-the-wall scores ≈ −1.36 (WORSE than into the wall, −1.2) and straight-away-from-target wins at −1.0 → perpendicular oscillation. The stuck-breaker (`moved < 2.0 px/tick`, bot-ai.js:382) never fires: a carrier moves 2.13 px/tick, so oscillating at full speed resets `stuck` every tick. It measures displacement, not progress toward the target.
  - **"Stands with the ball in front of the goal" — 3 stacked causes.** (1) The end line is boundary DANGER (m = r+34 = 55px, bot-ai.js:336) but the carrier's move target is `{egX, GY}` ON that line → it jitters ~55-75px out. (2) `laneWalls` (bot-ai.js:665) tests only the goal CENTRE and includes built walls; when false there is no finish, no corner finish, AND the anti-idle blast is also gated on it (bot-ai.js:752) → zero release path, so it drives into the wall. A full kick would DESTROY that wall (sim.js:1149) but **no "break the wall in my way" behaviour exists**, and the posts/corners are never tried as alternate aim points. (3) plus the 2.12s-vs-2.2s race above.
  - **"Too strong" — skill and cards double-dip off the same level.** `RARITY_BY_LEVEL` (server.js:1014) gives the best cards exactly where the skill vector spikes. L9 bot: chargeRate 2.05 × cardShot 1.25 = 2.56×; cdMul 0.55 × cardUtil 0.8 = 0.44; +20% speed; 90ms reaction; ~2× vision. Cheat tier compounds to **9.5× charge rate (full charge in 0.15s)** and super is PERMANENT (`if (sk.cheat && !p.power) p.power = true` re-grants every tick, bot-ai.js:522) + EXTREME_BOT_BUFFS +30% speed + x-ray + `preCharge` (~70% pre-wound = no telegraph). Only valve is `cheatFlub` (~16%, ~9° slip — negligible up close).
  - **The "sometimes" is real variance, bigger than a ladder step:** card rolls are `Math.random()` per match (server.js:1023, ±20% on speed/charge/cooldowns); `preCharge`/`cheatFlub` gate on a GLOBAL time bucket so bots pulse in sync; partner skill swings independently by level. Plus cliffs: cheat/preCharge snap on at t≥0.95, and 0.50→0.82 is the steepest interpolation stretch.
  - **Dead/wrong code spotted:** `staticCannonSpot` uses the global `ARENA` not `arenaOf(state)` (wrong geometry on builder fields, looks dead); `wallWouldPlace` always true; `finalize`'s `opts.hold` branch has no caller; `bm.decideAt/action/sitHash` unused.
  - **Big-game reference (per the standing rule):** Brawl Stars = tile-grid pathfinding + practice bots that never cheat (handicap = reaction delay + imperfect target selection). Fortnite = navmesh + whole-decision reaction budget, SBMM-tuned to measured skill not a progression counter. Roblox = PathfindingService/navmesh as the baseline for user-built geometry (= our field builder). Common thread: handicap PERCEPTION and REACTION, never grant vision/stats the player can't have.

- **🆕 NEW 3v3 MODE + bigger stadium + builder size presets (agent `3v3-arena`)** — User: "you work on a new 3v3 game, should allow 3v3 players, and make the stadium bigger. do research and use the council and other skills to decide how much bigger the stadium should be. also add option in the arena builder to build a bigger stadium for 3v3 (and maybe 5v5 in the future)."
  - Asks, short bullets: **(1)** new 3v3 mode, 3 players per team. **(2)** bigger stadium for it. **(3)** the size must be **researched + council-decided**, not guessed. **(4)** field builder gets a **bigger-arena option** for 3v3, forward-compatible with a future **5v5**.
  - **Hard constraints I verified before designing** (any agent picking this up: do not re-derive, but do re-check if you change these files):
    - `FIELD = {W:2000,H:1100}` is a module const in `shared/constants.js:6`, imported by sim + server + client → arena size is currently **global, not per-match**.
    - `shared/wire.js:37` packs positions as `P = i16(round(v*10))` → **hard ceiling ±3276.7 world px.** An arena wider than ~3200 (leaving camera headroom behind the goals) **overflows the wire**.
    - `shared/wire.js:51` player-presence mask is a **u8, loop `k < 8`** → **8 slots max. 3v3 (6) fits with NO wire change; 5v5 (10) does NOT.** That is the real cost of the future 5v5.
    - `public/client.js:4767` `scale = CAM_ZOOM * wbW / FIELD.W` → zoom is DERIVED from field width, so **a bigger arena silently zooms out and shrinks the players**. Must be pinned to a reference width, Brawl-style (pan more, don't zoom out).
    - `shared/sim.js:166` `spawnPos(team, slot)` only knows **slot 0/1** (`y = H*0.36 | H*0.64`) → needs a 3-per-side formula.
    - `MAX_PLAYERS = 4` (`shared/constants.js:316`), `TEAM_CAP = 2` (`server.js:75`).
    - Builder is pinned to `FB_W=2000, FB_H=1100, FB_GRID=50` (40×22 cells) and saves **absolute px with no size field** (`public/client.js:6469`, localStorage `pikme-field-v1`); `shared/field-presets.js` + `shared/main-field.js` use the same absolute shape → **plural sizes need a size field + a migration rule for existing saved fields.**
  - **Council run** (workflow `football-3v3-arena-council`, 11 agents): 3 web-research agents (Brawl Stars/Brawl Ball tile counts + area-per-player; real football 3v3/5-a-side/11v11 m² per player + Rocket League's one-arena-for-all-modes counter-example; Roblox/Fortnite editor bounds-change UX) + 3 codebase recon agents (size/roster coupling map, netcode+perf at 6 players, builder surface) → 3 sizing proposals (density / feel / systems lenses) → 2 adversarial judges (player + engineer) → 1 ruling.
  - **✅ USER DECISION — 3v3 STAYS ON 2000×1100. Do not re-open without him.** He was asked twice and chose it both times, the **second time after I showed him a correction that argued the other way**. Crowding at 3v3 is solved by LAYOUT (`shared/field-3v3.js`'s open central channel), not area. `GOAL`/`PENALTY` untouched.
  - **Council outcome** (12 agents, all 12 returned): Brawl Stars uses **one 21×33-tile canvas for every 3v3 mode across 400+ maps / 16 modes** — size is a function of player COUNT, never of mode (measured from 4 independent decompiled `maps.csv` dumps that agree). Across counts it grows superlinearly (3v3→5v5 Brawl Ball in-bounds area **×2.62**, ≈N^1.9); the FA's own 3v3 and 5v5 pitches are both exactly 100 m²/player. Rocket League's one-arena-for-all is **not transferable** — its cars cross in 4.5–7.3s, our players need **14.06s**. The presiding ruling wanted **2000×1300** (freeze W, grow H 18.2%) because visible world width is `FIELD.W/CAM_ZOOM` = **1212px on every device**, so WIDENING cancels the encounter density the 3rd player adds (expected on-screen opponents: 2v2 0.617 → 3v3@1100 **0.926** → @1300 0.783 → 2450×1350 **0.616**, which erases the third player exactly). User declined the H growth.
  - ⚠️ **TWO ARITHMETIC ERRORS THE COUNCIL ITSELF MADE — read before re-running this analysis.** (1) Density must use the **effective** radii: `defaultSettings()` ships `sizeMul: 1.25` + `ballSizeMul: 2`, so bodies are **52.5px** and the ball **64px**, not 42/32. Two agents disagreed and the wrong one reached the user first. Correct: 2v2 = 199.6 D²/player (1.32× Brawl Ball), 3v3@1100 = **133 (0.88× — TIGHTER than Brawl Stars ships)**, 3v3@1300 = 157 (1.04×). (2) The ruling's "**3v3 goal is sealed by 3 defenders**" is **NOT established** — the same method says 2v2 is sealed too (233px denial vs a 218px usable centre corridor) and 2v2 scores fine; it compared a centre-corridor denial against the full mouth width. Goal width at 3v3 is a **playtest** question, not arithmetic.
  - **🆕 SHIPPED (commit `3ad199f`) — builder can author a bigger stadium.** `shared/field-sizes.js` is the single source of truth: **s2v2** 2000×1100 (40×22 cells, reproduces the shipped constants to the pixel), **sBig** 2600×1500 (52×30), **sHuge** 2900×1700 (58×34). Every size is a whole AND **even** number of 50px cells on both axes (centre line stays a grid junction, builder mirror stays exact) and inside the wire's ±3276.7 range. Save format **v2 carries the size id INSIDE the field object**; **a size-less save is s2v2 forever and is never rescaled**.
  - **Three silent bugs fixed on the way:** (a) `sanitizeField` clamped every coord to the global `FIELD`, so a 2600-wide layout came back with its whole right-hand side **collapsed onto the touchline, no error** — it now clamps to the field's OWN size and echoes the resolved id. (b) **Four** separate `{version:1,…}` literals rebuilt the field object (`fbLoad`, `fbRestore`, `#b-clear`, `fpNormField`) and each **dropped the size** — all four now go through one `fbNorm()`, so the class of bug is gone, not just the instances. (c) shrinking a pitch used to be undefined; it is now **refused** while any element sits outside, because authored coordinates are never rewritten behind the author's back.
  - **`RUNTIME_SIZES = ['s2v2']` — sizes a MATCH can be hosted on ≠ sizes the builder can AUTHOR.** ▶ שחק refuses a non-hostable size AND `startBuilderMatch` refuses it again server-side; `test-builder-size.mjs` proves the client gate isn't trusted. **Flipping that one array is the whole switch** once stage 2 lands.
  - ⚠️ **STAGE 2 NOT DONE — playing a bigger pitch. BLOCKED, deliberately.** The pitch is read from **123 places** (33 `sim.js`, 25 `bot-ai.js`, 49 `client.js`, 11 `training.js`, 3 `server.js`, 2 `arena.js`), 11 computed at module load. **`bot-ai.js` holds 25 of them and the bot overhaul is actively rewriting it** (`00794e9`, "single-writer plan") → editing it would stomp that agent. Ordered plan, the 11 exact line refs, and the client-camera `REF_W` fix are in **`summery/ARENA-SIZES-PLAN.md`**.
  - **Files I took:** `shared/field-sizes.js`, `test-field-sizes.mjs`, `test-builder-size.mjs`, `summery/ARENA-SIZES-PLAN.md` (all new), `server.js`, `public/index.html`. **Suite: 52/53.** `test-mode-format.mjs` needs its own server (`PORT=3013 node server.js`) — green once served. `test-tricks.mjs` is **FLAKY, not broken**: it fails ~1 in 5 runs on `GIVE-AND-GO` (a statistical bot test with counters as low as 4 firings) and it's the bot agent's area.
  - ⚠️ **`public/client.js` + `public/style.css` builder hunks are NOT in `3ad199f`.** The pixel-clock agent had work in those same two files, so committing those paths would have swept it in. My hunks are the `📐 גודל` size cycler + `fbNorm`/`fbApplySize`/`fbOutOfBounds` (client.js ~6580–7060, plus the import) and the three `aspect-ratio: 2000 / 1100` → `var(--fb-aspect)` rules. **Saved as `summery/arena-sizes-builder-ui.patch.txt` (verified my-only, 0 pixel refs) so they're recoverable if a checkout eats them.** Whoever commits client.js next will carry them; that's fine, just know why the attribution is split.
  - **Latent bug found, still open, unrelated to sizes:** `shared/wire.js:69` packs wall AABBs as `u8(w.w); u8(w.h)` while the `u8` writer masks with `& 255` **without clamping** (the `hl`/`ht` bytes beside it ARE clamped). A vertical dry wall with `hl 115` has AABB h = 262 → `262 & 255` = 6, so it **renders 256px from where it collides**. Repro: tall dry wall in the builder.

- **🏆 TROPHIES + SKILL PROGRESSION — built (agent `trophy-lead`)** — User: "start building" against the signed-off spec (see the research entry below for the 4 locked decisions). Cross-repo: `pikme-server` + `football-mock` + `pikmeTV-saltiz`.
  - **Spec:** [`summery/research-trophies/00-DECISION.md`](summery/research-trophies/00-DECISION.md) — §3 numbers, §7 the user's binding decisions. Read it before changing any trophy number.
  - **Files I took:** NEW `shared/trophies.js`, `shared/techniques.js`, `shared/opponent-key.js`, `public/hub-trophies.js`, `public/trophies.css`, `test-trophies*.mjs`, `test-techniques.mjs`, `test-opponent-key.mjs`, `test-hub-trophies.mjs`. Small edits to `server.js` (matchStart), `public/client.js` (import + 1 render + 1 poll + reveal arm + a DEV_LOCAL preview), `public/index.html` (2 hunks: the stylesheet link + `#hub-trophies`).
  - **Backend (`pikme-server`, commits `4647dc0` `0fb04ec` `ec8dba3`):** `data/football-trophies.js` = all the math (banded win/loss/draw deltas, crossover at diamond, bronze+first-loss-of-day protection, the BOT CEILING, sticky `tierFloor`, daily bot cap, same-opponent win-trading cap, the legacy-XP migration seed). `footballstats` gained `trophies/trophiesPeak/tierFloor/trophyTier/trophiesSeeded` + the anti-farm counters. `/football/record-match` computes the delta from trusted fields only and now returns `{ xpDelta, trophyDelta, botCeiling }`. **`tier` (f(xp)) is untouched** so the live leaderboard keeps working; `trophyTier` is the new player-facing one.
  - **XP IS NOT DELETED — deliberately.** Every game we researched runs a losable ladder alongside a monotonic one. Trophies are the headline; XP still pays every match (so a loss is never all-negative) and still drives `botLevelFromXp` + unlocks. Don't "finish the job" by ripping XP out.
  - **BOT CEILING** (the load-bearing idea): `ceiling(L) = 60 + 80*L` per difficulty level. Bot matches pay **40%** below it and **0** at/above, and a bot win can never push you past it. So bots carry a solo player to ~940 and **the difficulty picker becomes the progression gate**; diamond+ is human-only. Verified end-to-end: stalls at 460 on L5 → raise to L11 → stalls at 940, badge capped at gold with 0 human wins.
  - **Skill progression = `shared/techniques.js`** — 6 training drills, bronze unlocks a technique (פיינט · בננה · בישול פצצה · דילוג קיר · חטיפה מדויקת · שרשור סופר). **HORIZONTAL ONLY** and the tests enforce it: `kind:'ability'`, no stat multipliers, unbuyable, not trophy-gated. Card powers are already the vertical axis. `activeEffects(state)` is the single seam the sim will read.
  - **⏳ NOT DONE — the sim/client hooks for those 6 technique effects.** The data model, unlock rules and drill definitions are done and tested; no gameplay behaviour is wired yet, so nothing is live in a match. That's the next slice and it's a real chunk (6 abilities × sim + wire + input + balance) — `shared/sim.js` and `shared/wire.js` will need locks.
  - **Tests:** `test-football-trophies.mjs` 117 · `test-techniques.mjs` 49 · `test-trophies.mjs` 38 · `test-hub-trophies.mjs` 36 (jsdom) · `test-opponent-key.mjs` · `test-trophies-parity.mjs` (**fails the build if the game's ladder drifts from the server's** — verified it detects injected drift). Full game suite green; `test-mode-format.mjs` needs a live `:3013` (pre-existing, flagged in `18c8158`).
  - **3 real bugs caught by writing the tests first:** (1) `Number(null) === 0`, so an unreported `botLevel` read as difficulty 0 (ceiling 60) → the "raise the difficulty" nudge would have shown to *every* player; (2) at max difficulty that nudge was a dead end (no higher level) → now says only human matches raise your rank; (3) an opponent key built from `member.id` would have been useless because ids are `m-<counter>`, fresh per connection — so the win-trading cap would have silently never fired. It's now server-computed per recipient from `userId` (also avoids leaking identities between players).
  - **Everything degrades gracefully on old app builds:** no `window.SALTIZ_TROPHIES` → the trophy layer stays hidden and the XP bar keeps its original slot (`.hub.has-trophies` gates the shift). No rush on TestFlight.
  - **Migration:** nobody resets to 0. `seedTrophiesFromXp` maps a player's *gated* xp tier onto the same trophy tier lazily on first read/record, guarded by `trophiesSeeded` (not `trophies === 0`, or a player legitimately at 0 would be re-seeded forever).

- **🏆 RESEARCH DONE, AWAITING SIGN-OFF — Trophies replace XP + a skill-progression mechanism (agent `rules-scribe`)** — User: "you work on the experience. I want trophies instead. Start 5 subagents to research best practices, if in doubt search Brawl Stars / Fortnite / Roblox. I want a new mechanism to progress player skills. How much from losing, from winning, from bots-only game etc. Do full research."
  - Ran 5 parallel research agents (web-sourced, ~1200 lines) → `summery/research-trophies/`: `01-brawl-stars.md` (trophy deltas/reset/Ranked/Mastery/bots), `02-other-games.md` (Fortnite Ranked, Rocket League MMR, Roblox UGC, Clash Royale, eFootball/FC Mobile + comparison table), `03-skill-progression.md` (Power Levels vs mastery vs technique unlocks, pay-to-win traps), `04-economy-bots-math.md` (PvE-vs-PvP currency rule, Elo/Glicko-2/TrueSkill, bot-as-rating-anchor, farm exploits), `05-psychology-migration-fit.md` (loss aversion, ranked anxiety in teens, migration cases, Hebrew/RTL UX).
  - **→ READ `summery/research-trophies/00-DECISION.md` FIRST** — the synthesis + the proposed spec.
  - Headline conclusions: (1) nobody ships a losable ladder ALONE — trophies become the visible number, the existing monotonic XP survives underneath as the every-match payout (keeps `computeMatchXp` + anti-rage-quit). (2) The agents split on whether bot matches pay trophies; resolved with a **BOT CEILING** — each `difficulty.js` level L has `ceiling = 60+80*L`, bot matches pay 40% up to it and 0 above, so bots can carry you to ~940 and the difficulty picker becomes the progression gate; Champion/Legend stays human-only (matches the live anti-farm gates). (3) Skill progression = **horizontal technique unlocks earned in graded training drills** (feint-cancel, curved shot, bomb-cook, wall-vault, precise strip) — never stat scaling, because card powers are already the vertical axis.
  - Proposed numbers are in `00-DECISION.md` §3 (per-band win/loss/draw, streak, first-loss-of-day protection, tier floors, daily + win-trading caps, no seasons at this playerbase).
  - **✅ USER SIGNED OFF on all 4 design questions — `00-DECISION.md` §7 is now BINDING.** (a) bot ceiling as proposed, 40% rate, bots cap at ~940, Champion/Legend human-only; (b) **no relegation** — sticky `tierFloor`, you never leave a tier you reached; (c) trophies big + XP as a thin secondary bar (XP keeps the every-match payout, `botLevelFromXp`, unlocks); (d) **trophies AND the training path ship TOGETHER in one release** — bigger scope, so build in the order backend math → techniques behind a flag → hub UI → app inject/TestFlight → migration, and **do not touch `shared/sim.js` while it's dirty**.
  - **NO CODE CHANGED** — docs only. Overlaps `modes-lead`'s lobby/modes pass below (trophy bar + tier badge live in the same hub UI) — coordinate before touching `public/client.js`.
  - Status: committed locally, **not pushed**. NEXT: implementation plan off `00-DECISION.md` §6+§7.

Headline decisions for the other 4 specs to build on: **exactly 2 public matchmade modes live at once** — 1 permanent (משחק מהיר 2v2) + **1 rotating "מצב היום" slot** flipping daily at 17:00 Asia/Jerusalem over a pool of [goal brawl · 3v3 · LTM]; everything private (party / challenge / training / builder) is unlimited because it brings its own players. **3v3 is party-first, NOT a third permanent queue** (needs 6 humans; as a queue it'd be a bot room with a bigger pitch) → tell `mode-3v3`. **Root cause of all-bot matches is `COUNTDOWN_TIME = 5` (server.js:71), not the mode count** — at our λ a 5s window gives ~4-15% chance of any second human; fix order is (1) persistent lobby / auto-rematch keeping humans across rounds, (2) adaptive 5s→~30s gathering window with a "התחל עכשיו" skip, (3) a `MODE_DEFS` data table replacing the five bespoke `start*()` paths + the `teamSlots={A:[0,1],B:[0,1]}` hardcode. Also: kill all 8 dead `בקרוב` cards, fix goal-brawl showing live in the hub strip but locked in `#arena`/`#party` (→ `lobby-picker`), never show player counts, global trophies only (no per-mode MMR), don't build clubs/tournaments/second currency/hard season resets.
  - **✅ ALL 5 SPECS DELIVERED** (3224 lines in `docs/specs/`, committed by `modes-lead`). Nothing implemented yet — these are specs, not code.
  - **CROSS-CUTTING FINDINGS — every agent independently hit these, treat as constraints:**
    - **`elapsed` is a u8 on the wire (`shared/wire.js:41`) → NO mode can exceed 4:15.** Binds overtime, 3v3 duration, and every new mode. Found by 3 of 5 agents separately.
    - **`fillBots` means our failure mode is an INVISIBLE queue, not a dead one** — a full match in ≤5s always, silently all-bot. The metric is `P(≥1 other human)`. Root cause `COUNTDOWN_TIME = 5` (`server.js:71`), NOT the mode count.
    - **Bots only understand football.** Any objective change makes them useless — fatal in a bot-filled game. This is the real filter on new modes.
    - **Do NOT add a third matchmaking pool.** `publicRoomBrawl` already forked the pool at M=2; `xpFactor` (0.2×–1.0× by human fraction) means splitting cuts everyone's XP.

  - **Architecture agreement across all 5 specs:** there are **five** hand-copied game-pick surfaces (hub rail, gold button, `#arena`, `#party`, `#game-select`) — collapse to one `MODES`/`MODE_DEFS` data table; that single change is what stops the picker bugs recurring AND unblocks per-mode team size / duration / geometry.

Costed "don't grow the pitch" as option Z′ (deletes the whole geometry refactor, cuts the spec by 2/3). Wire is size-agnostic (i16-tenths, ±3276.7px, 8 slots — byte-exact at 3v3, +2.0KB/s); the real risk is `GOAL_TOP`/`PENALTY_TOP` computed at MODULE LOAD in `sim.js:38-41`, `bot-ai.js:31-33`, `client.js:32-39` — miss one and it's a desync.

  - 🗄️ **WHERE THE DATA LIVES (this is the answer to "where is the db / what table"):**
    - **DB = MongoDB Atlas via Mongoose** — cluster `cluster0.gclzigv.mongodb.net`, database **`production`**, credentials from `process.env.mongo`. Set in `pikme-server/app.js:38-39`.
    - **Collection ("table") = `footballstats`** — `mongoose.model('footballstats', footballStatsSchema)` in `pikme-server/data/footballstats.js:266`. Keyed by unique indexed `phone`; `profileToken` = HMAC-SHA256(phone) truncated to 32 chars, so a third party can read PUBLIC stats without the phone ever leaking.
    - ⚠️ **Football is Mongo, NOT Postgres.** `pikme-server` also has a Postgres pool + `migrations/*.sql`, but those are for **messages / AI** only. Don't write football data there.
    - Related collections: `playercardstats` (cards), `FriendRequest` (friends).
  - ✅ **ALREADY STORED in `footballstats`:** `wins/losses/draws`, `winsVsHuman/winsVsBot`, `goalsFor/goalsAgainst`, `matchesPlayed`, `streak/bestStreak`, `xp/level/tier`, `trophies/trophyTier/trophiesPeak/tierFloor/trophiesSeeded`, `botTrophiesToday/opponentsToday`, `recordedMatchIds` (replay-dedupe), `cosmetic` ("hero:skin"), `loadout` (3 equipped cards), and the career tallies `careerGoals/assists/strips/saves/shotsFired/bombsPlanted/wallsBuilt/touches/possSeconds/distanceM`.
  - ✅ **ALREADY BUILT:** `GET /handle-user/football/stats?phone=…` (self, lazily creates the row) and `?token=…` (another player's public stats) in `routes-pikme/user.js` · `GET /handle-friends/rank` (live rank) in `routes-pikme/friends.js` · **and a profile screen: `pikmeTV-saltiz/app/pages/football-profile.jsx` + `football-profile.format.js` + tests, with a `הקריירה שלי` career section.**

    1. **Top hero** — `cosmetic` holds only the CURRENTLY equipped hero. There is no per-hero play counter, so "top hero" is **not derivable**. Needs a new field (e.g. `heroPlays: { striker: n, … }`) incremented at match end.
    2. **Cards stats** — `loadout` holds only the 3 currently equipped cards. No per-card usage / win-rate for football.

- **✅ DONE (localhost-verified, committed local, NOT pushed) — in-game connection / lag warnings (agent `rules-scribe-4`)** — User: "you work on the internet connection and in game alerts warnings. I want to show the user when his internet connection is unstable, laggy etc. When in doubt search what Brawl Stars / Fortnite / Roblox are doing." Design APPROVED by the user; spec committed at [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-connection-quality-warnings-design.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-connection-quality-warnings-design.md). Implementation not started yet.
  - **FILES I'M TAKING:** `public/net-quality.js` (new), `test-net-quality.mjs` (new), `public/client.js`, `public/index.html`, `public/style.css`. **NOT touching `server.js` or `shared/`** — so the 26-test suite and the other agents' in-flight `shared/sim.js` stats work are unaffected.
  - SCOPE (user chose all 3 recommended options): show the indicator **only when the connection is bad** (Brawl Stars style, nothing on screen when healthy) · **own connection only** this slice, no opponent-lag warning (that would need `server.js`+`wire.js`) · on a full stall **dim + spinner + `מתחבר מחדש…`, non-blocking**, match keeps running and local prediction keeps predicting.
  - SIGNALS (all already available client-side): existing ping/pong RTT (probe 1500→1000ms, ring buffer of 8) · **jitter** = mean abs deviation of that buffer · **snapshot gap** `now - lastSnapAt` (expected ~16.7ms at SNAPSHOT_RATE 60) — fastest freeze detector · `snapRate` vs 60 · **unacked inputs** `inputSeq - server.lastSeq` (catches UPSTREAM stalls) · `ws.readyState`.
  - ⚠️ **Transport reality — don't ask for a packet-loss %:** this is WebSocket = TCP, so lost packets are retransmitted, never missing at the app layer. Fortnite can show loss % because it's UDP; we can't, and the spec deliberately does NOT fake one. Loss shows up here as **jitter + burst gaps**, which is what the classifier keys on. Real loss stats only become possible if `summery/WEBRTC_TRANSPORT_PLAN.md` lands.
  - LEVELS: `offline` (ws closed) · `stalled` (gap>400ms) · `poor` (rtt>180 | jitter>60 | snapRate<30 | unacked>12) · `fair` (rtt>110 | jitter>30 | snapRate<48 | unacked>6) · `good`. Worst-first. **Hysteresis is the critical part** — ~600ms dwell before escalating, ~2500ms of good before hiding; `offline`/`stalled` skip the dwell. A strobing icon is worse than no icon.
  - UI: amber 2/3 wifi bars at `fair` → red 1/3 flashing + one-shot Hebrew toast `חיבור לא יציב` at `poor` (once per episode, auto-hide ~3s, does NOT nag) → dim+spinner overlay at `stalled`. Top-right by the timer, out of the joystick thumb zones. The old always-on dev chip (`${ping}ms · ${snapRate}/s`, client.js:5228) moves behind `?debug=net`.
  - VERIFY PLAN: `test-net-quality.mjs` unit tests (threshold boundaries, worst-first precedence, dwell both directions, oscillating input must NOT flap) + `?netsim=fair|poor|stalled` to force each visual state without wrecking a real network.
  - ✅ **SHIPPED:** `public/net-quality.js` (pure classifier + hysteresis) · `public/net-hud.js` (self-injecting HUD layer) · `test-net-quality.mjs` (46 assertions) · `test-net-hud.mjs` (jsdom DOM test) · **6 one-line hooks in `public/client.js`**. Commits `5e39910`, `7514944`, `d309005`, + the client wiring. **Suite 38/38 green.** Localhost-verified on `:3012` (modules serve 200, import graph resolves); server stopped afterwards.
  - 🔀 **DESIGN CHANGE vs the plan — `index.html` and `style.css` were NOT touched.** All three UI files were lock-held by other agents, so the HUD became a self-contained module that injects its own elements + `<style>`. Net footprint in shared files: **11 added lines in client.js, nothing else.** If you're looking for markup for the warning, it's in `net-hud.js`, not `index.html`.
  - 🧰 **How I committed into a file another agent was mid-edit** (reusable trick): `git diff -U0` → filter to only my hunks by content → `git apply --cached --unidiff-zero` → `git commit` (no `-a`). That staged my 11 lines and left their 336 uncommitted lines untouched. `git add -p` is unavailable (no interactive flags).
  - 🐛 **Two real bugs the jsdom test caught:** (1) `lastSnapAt` used `0` as the "never" sentinel, but `0` is falsy — a gap measured from t=0 silently reported no stall (now `null`). (2) the toast only hid on its 3s timer, leaving a stale "unstable" message over an already-recovered connection (now clears the moment the level improves, re-arms only after a full recovery).
  - 📋 **STILL NEEDS YOUR EYES:** visual QA on a device/browser — `?netsim=fair|poor|stalled` forces each state, `?debug=net` shows live `rtt ±jitter · rate · gap · queue · level`. Thresholds (fair 110ms / poor 180ms) are informed estimates, NOT measured against real Saltiz traffic; retune in `NET_T` in `public/net-quality.js`.
  - ⏭️ **DEFERRED:** opponent-lag warning (`היריב מתקשה בחיבור`) needs `server.js`+`wire.js`; real packet-loss % needs the WebRTC/UDP transport.

- **✅ FIXED — wall built in the WRONG position (agent `wall-place`)** — User: "sometimes the build wall is not building in the selected position — debug why and fix, plus suggest improvements; if in doubt search Brawl Stars / Fortnite / Roblox." 16-agent council (5 hypotheses × refuter + player-experience judge, 2 research agents, chair). Files taken: `server.js`, `public/client.js`, `shared/arena.js`, new `shared/input-merge.js` + `test-wall-place.mjs`. **`shared/sim.js` NOT touched** (was mid-edit by the stats agent). Commit **`2710141`**, **not pushed**.
  - **R1 — THE BUG: the input coalescer dropped the build's payload.** It latches `build` sticky and locks `aimX/aimY` to the FIRE edge and `sax/say` to the SPECIAL edge — but there was **no BUILD edge**: `buildDist: msg.buildDist || 0` took the newest packet, and `takeAim = !!msg.fire || !prev.fire` is TRUE for any non-firing packet. `releaseBuild()` edge-flushes P1{build, drag aim, buildDist}; the drag then resets so P2 carries {no-build, dist 0, stick aim}. Coalesced into one tick ⇒ the wall was built **in the direction you were RUNNING, at the minimum offset**. Same class as the shipped aim-latch fix (fire/special covered, build missed).
    - FIX: coalescer extracted to **`shared/input-merge.js`** (`coalesceInput` + `consumeEdges`) so it is testable. `takeBuild = !!msg.build || !prev.build` locks the build's aim + `buildDist`; `takeAim = !!msg.fire || (!prev.fire && takeBuild)` keeps **fire priority on the shared aim channel** — do NOT use the `!!msg.build || ...` variant, it lets a wall release steal a pending shot's direction (council proved it live). `consumeEdges` also clears `buildDist`, else the next tap-build inherits the last drag's reach.
    - MEASURED end-to-end over a real WebSocket, same harness both sides: **HEAD = 143.6px off and rotated 45° wrong (4/4 runs at a 0-4ms packet gap) → fixed = 2.4px, 0°** (residual is the player still moving during windup). At an 8-16ms gap the two packets land in separate ticks and even HEAD is fine — that is exactly why it was "sometimes". Council estimated 25-40% of touch drag-builds. Desktop `q` never hit it (no drag vector).
  - **R2 — the ghost lied near a line.** `buildWall()` clamps the wall centre into the pitch using the rotated AABB; the client ghost didn't clamp at all, so along a touchline the preview promised a spot the sim then slid up to ~160px away. Both sides now call one shared **`wallPlacement()`** in `shared/arena.js` (also kills the duplicated `Math.PI/16` literal). ⚠️ `sim.js buildWall()` still holds its own copy of the math — `test-wall-place.mjs` asserts the two agree across 126 position×angle×reach cases, so **when `shared/sim.js` is free, make `buildWall()` call `wallPlacement()`**.
  - **R3 — NOT fixed, different symptom, good pickup:** for ~0.35s after a build the sim pins `buildWindup = 0` for the whole `buildCd`, while the client ring is pure wall-clock → full gold ring + confirm sound + **no wall and no feedback**. Client-only fix: gate the local ring on the server's `buildCd`/ammo (`updateBuildHud`, `public/client.js`). The sim-side alternative touches `shared/sim.js`.
  - **REJECTED by the council** (measured, not guessed): wire byte round-trip (worst 0.6px), AABB-vs-capsule render mismatch, wall interpolation (walls draw from the raw snapshot, `INTERP_DELAY` doesn't apply), player drift during windup (1-3.5px — the sim halves speed while holding), and angle quantization itself (ghost vs sim already agreed to 0.000px / 0.0000° in open field).
  - **IMPROVEMENTS suggested, none applied** (from the Brawl Stars / Fortnite / Roblox research): stream the build aim on every windup packet so direction survives a dropped packet (also fixes the enemy "winding" telegraph pointing the wrong way) · keep the committed ghost on screen until the real wall arrives, then tween ~120ms · fix the cancel-hysteresis trap (a re-aim sweep through the button centre silently cancels in the 12-34px band) · detented reach with tick marks + haptic click · `ownSpeed()` should apply the windup slow so the predicted hero (and the ghost origin) stops running 4-38px ahead · draw the ghost as its 4 blocks tinted by fragility. **Long-term:** promote action edges to a per-player **command queue** instead of merging into one mutable input — fire was patched, special was patched, build was missed, and the next edge will be missed the same way.

- **Built wall → 4 INDEPENDENT blocks (5-agent council + 10+ what-ifs) (agent `wall-4block`)**
  - Design goal (user): a built wall must act as ONE continuous barrier, but the part crossing a restricted zone is weaker — knock out only that part, the solid part holds. REPLACES the old "one piece, fragile only if ALL 4 sampled sections restricted".
  - `shared/sim.js` buildWall: one build now pushes `WALL_BLOCKS` (4) block-capsules that TILE the length (centres spaced 2·blockHl → abut, no gap/overlap), all sharing one `wallId`. Each block's fragility is its OWN zone: bush/penalty block = FRAGILE hp1, open-ground block = SOLID hp3. Per-block AABB via `capsuleAABB` (byte-consistent w/ hardWall). `WALL_BLOCKS` now exported.
  - Eviction is GROUP-aware: MAX_BUILT_WALLS counts distinct wallId groups; over cap evicts the WHOLE oldest group (never leaves a mutilated 3-block wall).
  - Wire: NO change — 4 blocks = 4 normal wall entries; `wallId` is server-only. Client renders each block as a rect slab (`fillRect`) so 4 abutting blocks tile into one seamless bar; fragile blocks already render glassy → you SEE which segment is weak. `client.js` drawWallPips: pips now ONLY on a DAMAGED block (no 12-pip clutter on a pristine solid wall; a dinged segment shows exactly where the wall is hurt).
  - Verified: 28-check what-if harness ALL PASS — open (SSSS hp3, blocks abut, one wallId), full bush (FFFF hp1), half bush (mixed, top weak/bottom solid), penalty (fragile), opponent-overlap (enemy does NOT weaken → SSSS), covering a steel wall (4 solid coexist w/ steel), angled half-bush (partial), light-damage (fragile crumble, solid HOLD, survivors keep wallId), eviction (8 groups=32 blocks, oldest whole group gone, no mutilated groups).
  - Tests updated to the 4-block reality: test-build-windup + test-mechanics count assertions (1 wall → WALL_BLOCKS blocks + one-wallId check). Full suite 24/26; the 2 fails (test-power, test-party) are PRE-EXISTING/unrelated (test-power stale vs committed shot-rebalance — fails on clean HEAD too; test-party needs a live :3010 server).

- **Wall health → SHARED per ZONE (single-unit behavior) (agent `wall-4block`)**
  - User: the wall must act as ONE unit — if half is on a penalty the SOLID half keeps FULL health and only the penalty part breaks off. The prior ship made all 4 blocks fully independent (each solid block its own hp3), which did NOT read as one unit.
  - `shared/sim.js` damageWall: damage now shares across same-ZONE blocks of a wallId group. SOLID blocks share one hp3 pool; WEAK (bush/penalty) blocks share one hp1 pool. A hit on any solid block drains ALL solid blocks together (they hold, then fall, as one); a hit on the weak zone breaks it off independently without touching the solid health. Field dry walls (no wallId) still damage individually. (This also makes the existing bomb comment at ~L1350 true.)
  - New `test-wall-shared.mjs` (14 checks, end-to-end via REAL shots): tap on solid → all solid blocks -1 (weak untouched); solid zone falls together after hp taps (weak stays); tap on weak → weak zone breaks, solid stays at FULL health; full shot drops the whole solid zone in one hit; all-open wall is one solid unit. Full suite 25/27 (same 2 pre-existing fails).

TestFlight was already correct because its WebView loads the game from Render (live).
